| ADR-033 | Cross-family cognitive-diversity guarantee: Decision 1 — amend adversary/spec-reviewer/code-reviewer agent descriptions to stop asserting cross-family model dispatch they do not perform (all resolved to Claude under n=3 live dispatch evidence 2026-07-27); honest same-family (Claude) disclosure with opt-in cross-family path when `VSDD_CROSS_FAMILY_DISPATCH=enabled`; Decision 2 — cross-family mechanism when enabled: `agy --print --model gemini-2.5-flash` via STDIN-pipe; adversary/spec-reviewer/code-reviewer roles when configured; authentication preflight mandatory; fail-loud on auth failure; Decision 3 — annotate historical convergences with ADR-033 disclosure block; do NOT invalidate prior convergences; only human can authorize retroactive invalidation; active cascades (S-21.04 streak 0/3) may continue same-family with disclosure; Decision 4 — four silent-failure classes: (a) D-927 falsified — `model: opus` resolves correctly to `claude-opus-5`; no agent repin needed; (b) `VSDD_CROSS_FAMILY_DISPATCH=enabled` + auth failure MUST halt with loud error, no silent fallback; (c) all review artifacts MUST include `model_family:` + `model_id:` frontmatter; (d) `handoff-validator` + `validate-pr-review-posted` SubagentStop gates MUST surface block reason to transcript. Story stubs: S-22.01 P0 (agent description corrections), S-22.02 P0 (provenance frontmatter + SubagentStop transcript surfacing), S-22.03 P1 (agy opt-in dispatch; depends_on S-22.01). **ACCEPTED 2026-07-27; ADR-033 v1.0.** | SS-05, SS-06 | decisions/ADR-033-cross-family-cognitive-diversity-guarantee.md |
| ADR-029 | Dispatcher git-context payload injection for WASM chain-detection gates — Option A (human-approved): dispatcher (host) execs git on PostToolUse Bash git-commit events; injects `git_context: {head_subject, head_sha, head_parent_subject, head_parent_sha}` into payload.extra; WASM plugins read payload field instead of calling exec_subprocess; removes PC1 violation in validate-burst-log + validate-dispatch-advance check_factory_artifacts_chain(); HOST_ABI_VERSION remains 1; trigger moves from Edit/Write PostToolUse to Bash PostToolUse (git commit detection); prerequisite story S-18.04b-prereq for dispatcher injection side; S-18.04b for WASM plugin rewiring; vp084-proof.bats schema corrected to tool="Bash" + head_sha/head_parent_sha fields; **Decision 8 (v1.2): S-18.04b LOCAL adversarial cascade F-P1-001 adjudication — mutation test found bats positive tests tautological for exemption decision (real PreCompact subjects are non-sentinel; block cannot fire regardless of exemption); architect determination: pure-Rust unit tests in `exemption.rs` (Section 1) are the load-bearing proof vehicle for `is_precompact_flush_exempt` 3-case decision; bats integration tests prove dispatcher injection plumbing + end-to-end chain-detection discrimination (negative-control Test 3 is mutation anchor); exemption is NOT dead code (defense-in-depth against future sentinel-set broadening); no production code change; BC-5.41.003 PC4 reworded (two-layer proof); VP-084 Feasibility Assessment updated (two-layer architecture)** — **PROPOSED 2026-06-24; ADR-029 v1.3; E-18 (CAP-032); SS-01 (dispatcher host) + SS-04 (WASM plugin consumer)** | SS-01, SS-04 | decisions/ADR-029-dispatcher-git-context-payload-injection.md |
| ADR-028 | E-18 precompact-flush native WASM migration — runtime worktree discovery and native lock renewal: (1) factory-artifacts worktree path discovered at runtime via `exec_subprocess("git", &["worktree","list","--porcelain"])` — parse for `refs/heads/factory-artifacts` stanza; no hard-coded path; no registry config key; absent-worktree → exit 0 advisory; (2) lock renewal native Rust in WASM via `crates/factory-lock::renew_lock()` — no bash exec; `binary_allow = ["git"]` ONLY; semantically faithful to `factory-lock-write.sh renew`: preserve `holder`+`locked_at`, update `expires_at = now + 2700s`; no-op when lock absent; fail-open (advisory) on malformed block; (3) new shared library crate `crates/factory-lock` (lib-only; no `[[bin]]`; no WASM output; depends on `crates/factory-lock-parse`; provides `renew_lock/acquire_lock/clear_lock`; reusable by future native WASM plugins); (4) canonical execution order: native renew (step 3) before git add (step 4) — renewed `expires_at` included in flush commit; (5) standing ADR-014 policy: native WASM only for new hooks; `binary_allow = ["git"]` no bash; bash `factory-lock-write.sh` retained for agent/skill callers (ADR-025 §D11 Mechanism 1); BC-6.23.001 + S-17.04 NO amendment needed; BC-7.07.001 amendment required (PC1 registry stanza → native WASM; PC3/INV3-step3 → native Rust; INV6 → Rust error handling; Arch Anchors → WASM crate + crates/factory-lock); S-18.04a amendment required (target_module → Rust crate; file list → Rust deliverables; ACs for worktree discovery + native renewal + Red Gate cargo unit + bats) — **ACCEPTED 2026-06-20; ADR-028 v1.0.
